Subject:  Submission of the public announcement dated November 25, 2026 ("Public 
Announcement") for the buyback of fully paid-up equity shares of face value of 
INR 2/- (Indian Rupees Two only) each ("Equity Shares") by Dalmia Bharat Limited 
("Company") from the open market through the stock exchange mechanism, 
pursuant to the provisions of the Securities and Exchange Board of India (Buy-
Back of Securities) Regulations, 2018, as amended ("Buyback Regulations") 
("Buyback") 
 
 
Dear Sir/Madam, 
 
This is further to our letter dated November 23, 2026, intimating the outcome of the meeting 
of the Board of directors of the Company (“Board”) held on the same date, which approved, 
inter alia, the Buyback of Equity Shares from the open market through the stock exchange 
mechanism. 
 
In this connection, pursuant to Regulation 16(iv) of the Buyback Regulations, the Company 
has made a public announcement dated November 25, 2026 for the Buyback, which was 
published on November 26, 2026, in the following newspapers:

and 
b. Certified true copy of the resolution passed by the Board on November 23, 2026, 
approving the Buyback (Annexure 2).

Auditor's Responsibility 
5. 
Pursuant to the requirements of lhe SEBI buy-back regulations, it Is our 
responsibility 10 provide reasonable assurance on whelher: 
a) 
we have inquired Into the stale of affairs of the Company in relation to the 
audited consolidated and standalone financial statement~ for the year ended 
31 March 2026: 
b) 
lhe amount of permissible capital paymen~ as slated in the Statement, 
has been properly determined considering the audited consolidated anci 
standalone financial statements for the year ended 31 March 2026 in 
accordance wilh section 68(2)(b) and 68(2)(c) of the Act and Regulation 4(iv) 
and the proviso to regulation S(i)(b) of the SEBI buy-back regulations; and 
c) 
whether Ille Board of Directors of Ille Company, In Its meeting dated 23 
November 2026, has formed the opinion as specified in clause (x) of 
Schedule I to Ille SEBI buy-back regulations, on reasonable grounds and 
that the Company will not, having regard to its state of affairs, be rendered 
insolvent within a period of one year from the aforesaid date. 
6. 
The audited consolidated and standalone financial statements, referred to in 
paragraph 5 above, have been audited by us, on which we have issued unmodifieq 
audit opinion vide our report dated 28 April 2026. Our audits of lhese financial 
statements was conducted in accordance with the Standards on Auditing 
specified under Section 143(10) of tne Companies Act, 2013 and other applicable 
authoritative pronouncements Issued by the Institute of Chartered Acoountants of 
India (the 'ICAI'). Those Standards require that we plan and perform the audtt to 
obtain reasonable assurance about whether the financial statements are free of 
material misstatement. Such audit was not planned and performed in connection 
with any transactions to identify matters that may be of potential interest to lhird 
parties. 
7. 
We conducted our examination of the Statement in accordance with lhe 'Guidance 
Note on Reports or Certificates for Special Purposes· ('Guidance Note'), Issued by 
the ICAI. The Guidance Note requires that we comply with Ille ethical requirements 
of the Code of Ethics issued by the ICAI. 
8. 
We have complied with the rel~vant applicable requirements or the Standard 
on Qualily Control (SQC) 1. Qualily Control for Firms that Perform Audits and 
Reviews of Historical Financial Information, and Other Assurance and Related

DATE OF BOARD APPROVAL FOR BUYBACK 
13.1 
The Buyback has been approved by lhe Board at lhe Board Meeting held on 
November 23, 2026. Since the Maximum Buyback Size is less than 10% of the 
total paid-up equity share capilal and free reserves of the Company based on both 
standalone and consolidated financial statements as at March 31, 2026, approval 
of the shareholders of the Company is not required In acoordance wilh the proviso 
10 Section 68(2)(b) of the Companies Act and lhe proviso to Regula.tion S(i)(b) of

MINIMUM AND MAXIMUM NUMBER OF EQUITY SHARES PROPOSED TO 
BE BOUGHT BACK, SOURCES OF FUNDS AND COST OF FINANCING 
THE BUYBACK 
14.1 
Al the Maximum Buyback Size and the Maximum Buyback Price, the Indicative 
maximum number of Equity Shares bought back would be 37,83,783 (Thirty Seven 
Lakh Eighty Three Thousand Seven Hundred and Eighty Three) Equity Shares. If 
the Equity Shares are bought back al a price below lhe Maximum Buyback Price, 
the actual number of Equity Shares bought back could exceed lhe Maximum 
Buyback Shares (assuming full deployment of the Maximum Buyback Size), but 
will always be subject to the Maximum Buyback Size and will not exceed 25% 
of the lotal number of Equity Shares in the paid-up equity share capital of the 
Company. 
14.2 
The actual number of Equity Shares bought back will depend upon lhe actual 
price, excluding Transaction Costs. paid for the Equity Shares bought back and 
the aggregate consideration paid in the Buyback, subject to the Maximum Buyback 
Size. 
14.3 
Further. in accordance with Buyback Regulations, the Company shall utilise at 
least 75% of the Maximum Buyback Size for the Buyback i.e., INR 525,00,00,000l-
(lndian Rupees Five Hundred and Twenty Five Crore only) (i.e., the Minimum 
Buyback Size). Based on the Minimum Buyback Size and the Maximum Buyback 
Price, the Company will purchase an indicative minimum of 28,37,837 (Twenty 
Eight Lakh ThirtY Seven Thousand Eight Hundred and Thirty Seven) Equity Shares. 
Further, at least 40% of the Maximum Buyback Size. I.e., INR 280,00,00.0001-
(lndian Rupees Two Hundred and Eighty Crore only) shall be utilised within lhe 
initial half of the Maximum Buyback Period. 
14.4 
The Buyback will be funded out of the free reserves and/ or the securities premium 
account of the Company and/ or such other sources as may be permitted by the 
Buyback Regulations and the Companies Act, and on such tenns and conditions 
as the Board may deem fit. Borrowed funds from banks and flnanclal inslilutions 
will not be used for the Buyback. In terms of Section 69 of the Companies Act, lhe 
Company shall transfer from Its free reserves and/ or securities premium account 
a sum equal to the face value of the Equity Shares bought back lo lhe capital 
redemption reserve account, and the detaiis of such transfer shall be disdosed in 
its subsequent audited financial statements.

PROCESS AND METHODOLOGY TO BE ADOPTED FOR THE BUYBACK 
The Buyback is open to all shareholders/ beneficlal owners of the Company holding 
Equity Shares in dematerialised form, other than the Promoter Group, who shall not 
participate in the Buyback in terms of Regulation 16(ii) of the Buyback Regulations. 
Further, as required under the Companies Act and the Buyback Regulations, the 
Company will not purchase Equity Shares which are partly P.aid-up, Equity Shares 
with calls-in-arrears, locked-in Equity Shares or no,>-transferable Equity Shares, 
until they become fully paid-up, or until lhe pendency of such lock-In. or until such 
Equity Shares become freely transferable. as applicable. 
The Buyback will be implemented by the Company byway of open market purchases 
through the Stock Exchanges. by the order matching mechanism except lhe "all 
or none• order matching system, as provided under the Buyback Regulations. 
and the price-time priority based order matching principle as provided under the 
circulars issued by lhe Stock Exchanges, In accordance with the Securities and 
Exchange Board of India (Buy-Back of Securities) (Amendment) Regulations,

16.6 
Procedure for Buyback of Demat Shares: Beneficial owners holding Oemat 
Shares who desire to sell their Equity Shares in the Buyback would have to do 
so through their stock broker, who is a registered member of eiiher of the Stock 
Exchanges, by indicating to their broker the details of the Equity Shares they intend 
to sell whenever the Company has placed a "buy" order. The Company shall place 
a "buy" order by indicating to the Company's Broker the number of Equity Shares it 
intends to buy along with a price for the same. The trade would be executed at the 
price al which the order matches the price offered by the beneficial owner, and that 
price would be the Buyback price for that beneficial owner. The execution of the 
order. Issuance of contract note, delivery of the stock and receipt of payment would 
be carried out by the Company's Broker in accordance with the requirements of 
the Stock Exchanges and SEBI. Orders can be placed on lhe trading days of the 
Stock Exchanges and the Company is under no obligation to place. a "buy· order 
on a daily basis.

Extingulshment of Demat Shares: The Demat Shares bought back shall be

BRIEF INFORMATION ABOUT THE COMPANY 
18.1 
The Company was Incorporated on July 12, 2013, under the Companies Act, 1956. 
The CIN of the Company is L 14200TN2013PLC112346 and its registered office is 
at Dalmiapuram, Lalgudi, Dist. Tiruchirappalli, Tamil Nadu-621651, India. 
18.2 
Brief description of business, subsidiaries and key brands/ operations: 
Founded in 1939, Dalmia Bharat Limited is one of India's leading cement 
manufacturers, headquartered in New Delhi. With a legacy spanning over 85 (eighty 
five) years, the Company has been at the forefront of innovation, sustainability, and 
nation~building in the cement industry. With an installed cement capacity of 54.7 
MnT, the Company is the fourth-largest cement manufacturer in India. Company 
and Its subsidiaries operate 19 (nineteen) slate-of-the-art manufacturing units 
across 12 (twelve) states and, supported by a robust network of approximately 
49,700+ channel partners, serves customers across more than 23 (twenty three) 
states. The Company is globally recognized for tts leadership in the clean energy 
transition and has one of the lowest carbon footprints in the cement industry. 11 
aspires to become the world's first carbon negative cement company by the year 
2040 and has taken significant steps towards achieving this·goal. The Company 
is also the first Indian cement company to Join lhe Climate Group's all three of 
global initiatives - RE100, EP100, and EV100 underscoring iis commitment to 
100% renewable energy, energy efficiency and electric mobility. Research 
and development (R&D) remains a priority. With the Company operating lhree 
cutting-edge R&D centers, including robotic labs, to drive innovation in cement 
production and sustainability. By continuously investing in advanced technology 
and sustainable solutions, the C.ompany reinforces its leadership position in the 
industry. 
The Company sells Its cement in brands Including Oalmla Bharat Cement DC, 
Oalmia Bharat Cement OSP, Dalmia Bharat Cement Weather365, Dalmia Bharat 
Cement Supreme, Konark and Dalmia Bharat Cement lnfraPro. The Company has 
played a pivotal role in shaping India's modern infrastructure by contributing to 
some of the country•s most Iconic projects, Including Shuttle by Dafmia Bharat at 
Kallnga Stadium in Bhubaneswar, Hirakud Dam in Odisha, Chennal Metro, Salt 
Lake Stadium in Kolkata, Dhola Sadiya Bridge In Assam (India's longest bridge) 
and the Hollongi Airstrip in Arunacnal Pradesh. 
The Company has 31 (thirty one) subsidiaries, including 2 (two) material 
subsidiaries, namely Dalmia Cement (Bharat) Limited and Dalmia Cement (North 
East) Limited.

INTENTION OF MEMBERS OF THE PROMOTER GROUP AND PERSONS 
IN CONTROL OF THE COMPANY TO PARTICIPATE IN THE BUYBACK 
In terms of Regulation 16(il) of lhe Buyback Regulations. since lhe Buyback is being 
implemented by way of open market purchases through the Stock Exchanges. the 
Buyback shall not be extended to the promoters, promoter group and persons in 
conlrol of the Company. 
Further. in compliance with Regulation 24(i)(e) of the Buyback Regulations. the 
Promoter Group and their associates shall not deaf In lhe Equity Shares or olher 
specified securities of the Company either through the Stock Exchanges or off• 
market transactions {including inter·se transfer of Equity Shares amongst them) 
during the period from the date of the Board Mealing till the closing of the Buybaci<. 
In addition to the above. as per Regulation 24{i)(ea) of the Buyback Regulations, 
the Equity Shares held by lhe Promoter Group, Including their associates, shall 
remain frozen at the lnfe.mational Securities Identification Number {ISIN) tevel 
during the period from the date of the Board Meeilng till the closing of the Buyback. 
The transfer of Equity Shares pursuanl 10 invocation of encumbrances created 
prior to the commencement of the Buyback may be allowed. subject to the 
freeze continuing lo apply to such Equity Shares and to such conditions as may 
be specified by SEBI, The Company has provided necessary instrucfions to the 
depositories to give effect to such freezing, in accordance with the SEBI circular 
no. H0/49/i4l13(11)2026-CFD-POD1/l/1686412026 dated July 21, 2026 and the 
operational guidelines issued by the depositories. 
CONFIRMATIONS FROM THE COMPANY AS PER THE PROVISIONS OF 
THE BUYBACK REGULATIONS AND THE COMPANIES ACT 
All the Equity Shares of the Company are fully paid up, 
The Equity Shares of the Company are frequently traded shares in terms of the 
Buyback Regulations. 
The Buypaci< will be implemented by the Company by way of open market 
purchases through the Stock Exchanges. by order matching mechanism except 
"all or none" order matching system, as provided under the Buyback Regulations. 
The Company shall not Issue any Equity Shares or other specined secorllies 
(including by way of bonus) from the date of the Board Meeting till the expiry 
of the Buyback period. except in discharge of its subsisting obJigations through 
conversion of warrants. stock option schemes, sweat equity Of conversion of 
preference shares or debentures into Equity Shares. 
Unless othervilse specifically permitted by any relaxation Issued by SEBI and/ 
or any other regulatory authority, the Company shall not raise further capital for 
a period of 1 (one) year from the expiry of the Buyback period, as prescribed 
under Regulation 240)(1) of lhe Buyback Regulations, except in discharge of ils 
subsisting obligations. 
The Company. as per the provisions of Section 68(8) of the Companies Acl, will 
not make a further issue of the same kind of shares or other securities induding 
allotment of new shares under Section 82(1 )(a) or Olher specified s=rities within 
a period of 6 (six) months after the completion of the Buyback except by way of 
a bonus issue or in the discharge of subsisting obligations such as conversion of 
warrants, stock option schemes, sweat equily or conversion of preference shares 
or debentures Into equity shares. 
The Company shall not withdraw the Buyback after this Public Announcement has 
been made. 
The Company will ensure consequent reduction of its share capital post Buyback 
and the Equity Shares bought back by the Company will be extinguished and 
destroyed in the manner prescribed under the Buyback Regulations and the 
Companies Act within the specined ilmelines.

back by it to the Stock Exchanges on adeily basis, In accOfdance with the Buyback 
Regulations and In such form as may be prescribed by SEBI, and shall also upload 
such information on its website (www.dalmiabharat.com l on a daily basis.

bought back through the Buyback to the capital redemption reserve acoount and 
the details of such transfer shall be disclosed in its subsequent audited financial 
statement. 
10.29 The Company shall not undertake the Buyback unless it has obtained pnor consent 
ohts lenders, in case of breach of any covenant with such lenders. The Company 
confirms that the covenants with its lenders are not being bre3ched pursuant to 
the Buyback and accordingly lhe Company does not require any consenl from lls 
lenders for undertaking the Buyback.

THE BUYBACK 
14.1 
At lhe Maximum Buyback Size and the Maximum Buyback Price, the indicative 
maximum number of Equity Shares bought back would be 37,83,783 (Thirty Seven 
lakh Eighty Three Thousand Seven Hundred and Eighty Three) Equi1y Shares. If 
the Equity Shares are bought back at a price below the Maximum Buyback Price, 
the aclual number of Equity Shares bought back could exceed the Maximum 
Buyback Shares (assuming full deploymen, of tho Max.lmum Buyback Siz:e), but

Price, the Company will purchase an Indicative minimum of 28,37,837 (Twenty

Buyback Regulations and the Companies Act, and on such terms and conditions 
as the Board may deem fit. Borrowed funds from banks and financial institutions

a sum equal to the face value of the Equity Shares bought bad< to the capi,al

PROCESS AND METHODOLOGY TO BE ADOPTED FOR THE BUYBACK 
The Buyback Is open to all shareholders/ beneficial owners of the Company holding 
Equity Shares in dematerialised form, other than the Promoter Group. who shaJI not 
participate in the Buyback in terms of Regulation 16{ii) of the Buyback Regulations. 
Further. as required under the Companies Ac1 and the Buyback Regulations. the 
Company will not purchase Equity Shares which are partly paid~up, Equity Shares 
with calls-in-arrears, locked·ln Equity Shares or non·lransferable Equity Shares, 
until they become fully paid-up, or until the pendency of such lock-In, or until such 
Equity Shares become freety transferable, as applicable. 
The Buyback will be Implemented by the Company byway of open market purchases 
through the Stock Exchanges, by the order matching mechanism except the "all 
or none' order matching system. as provided under the Buyback Regulations. 
and the price-time priority based order matching principle as provided under the 
circulars issued by the Stock Exchanges, in accordance with the Securities and 
Exchange Board of India (Buy-Back of Securities) (Amendment) Regulations.

Account will be extinguished on or before the 15• (fifteenth) day ol the month 
succeeding the month in which such Equity Shares are bought back, provided that 
all Dema1 Shares bought back are extinguished within 7 (seven) Working Days 
from the expicy of the Buyback period. 
17.3 
Consideration for the Equity Shares bought back by the Company shall be paid 
only by way of cash. 
18 
BRIEF INFORMATION ABOUT THE COMPANY 
18.1 
The Company was incorporated on Juty 12, 2013, under the Companies Act, 1956. 
The CtN of the Company is L 14200TN2013PLC112346 and its registered office is 
at Dalmiapuram, Lalgudi, Dist. Tiruchirappatli, Tamil Nadu - 621 651. India. 
18.2 
Brief description of business, subsidiaries and key brands/ operations: 
Founded in 1939. OaJmia Bharat Limited is one of India's leading cement 
manufacturers, headquartered in New Deihl. With a legacy spanning over 85 (eighty 
five) years, the Company has been at the forelront or innovation, sustainability. and 
nation-building In the cement industcy. With an installed cement capacity .of 54.7 
MnT, the Company is the fourth-largest cement manufacturer in India. Company 
and its subsidiaries operate 19 {nineteen) state-of•th&-art manufacturing units 
across 12 (twelve) states and. supported by a robust network of approximately 
49,700+ channel partners. serves customers across more than 23 (twenty three) 
states. The Company is globally recognized for its leadership in the clean energy 
transition and has one of the lowest carbon footprints in the cement lndust,y. It 
a.spires to become the world's first carbon negative cement company by the year 
2040 and has taken significant steps towards achieving this goal. 'The Company 
is also the nrst Indian cement company to join me Climate Group's all three of 
global initiatives - RE100, EP100, and EV100 underscoring Its commitment to 
100% renewable energy, energy efficiency and electric mobility. Research 
and development (R&D) remains a priority, with the Company operating three 
cutting~dge R&D centers, including robotic labs, to drive innovation in cement 
production and sustalnability. By oontinuously investing In advanced technology 
and sustainable solutions, the Company reinforces its leadership position in the 
industry. 
The Company sells its cemenl in brands Including Oalmia Bharat Cement DC, 
Dalmia Bharat Cement DSP, Dalmia Bharat Cemen1 Weather365, Dalmla Bharat 
Cement Supreme, Konark and Dalm,a Bharat Cemenl lnfraPro. The Company has 
played a pivo1a1 role in shaping India's modern infrastructure by contributing to 
some of the country's most iconic projects, including Shuttle by Dalmia Bharat at 
t<aJinga Stadium in Bhubaneswar, Hirakud Dam in Odisha, Chennai Metro. Salt 
lake Stadlum in Kolkata, Dhola Sadiya Bridge In Assam (India's longest bridge) 
and the Hollongl Alrstrlp In Arunachal Pradesh. 
The Company has. 31 (thirty one) subsidiaries, including 2 (two) material 
subsidiaries. namely Dalmia Cement (Bharat) Limited and Dalmia Cement (North 
East) Limited.

DETAILS OF ESCROW ACCOUNT AND THE AMOUNT DEPOSITED 
THEREIN 
In accordance with Regulation 20 of the Buyback Regulations and towards security 
for performance of Its obligations thereunder, the Company has entered into an 
escrow agreement dated November 25, 2026 (~Escrow Agreement") with the 
Manager to the Buyback and Axis Bank Limited, which has been appointed as 
the eS-O(ow bank f'Escrow Bank"), pursuant to which !he Company has opened 
an escrow account in the name and style ·oALMIA SHARA T LIMITED-ESCROW 
ACCOUNT -BUYBACK 2026" ('Escrow Accounr), The Company has aulllorised 
the Manager 10 the Buyback to operate the Escrow Account In compliance wHh 
the Buyback Regulations and the Escrow AgreemenL The Company will, within 
2 (two) Wor1<ing Days from the date of this Public Announcement, in accordance 
wilh Regolatlon 20(i) of the Buyback Regola1ions, deposit a sum equivalent to 
2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size in the Escrow Account and arrange for a 
bank guarantee In favour of the Manager to the Buyback, which together with the 
cash deposited in the ES-O(OW Account makes up lhe requisite escrow ;,mount 
under the Buyback Regulations being 25% of the Maximum Buyback Size 
('E.scrow Amount"). Further, such bank guarantee will be valid (i) for a period of 
30 (thirty) days after the expiry of the Buybaci< Period, or (ii) till the completion of 
all obligations of the Company under the Buyback Regulations, whichever is later. 
The funds in the Escrow Account may be released ror making payment to the 
shareholders, subject to at least 2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size, i.e. INR 
17,50.00.000/- (Indian Rupees Seventeen Crore Fifty Lakh only) remaining in 
Ille Esc,ow Account at all points in time. The balance lying to the credit of the 
Escrow Account will be released to the Company in compliance with the Buyback 
Regulations. 
If the Company is unable to complete the Buyback equivalent to the Minimum 
Buyback Size, I.e., INR S25.00,00.000/- (Indian Rupees Five Hundred and Twenty 
Five Crore only) or to utilise at least 40% of the Maximum Buyback Size, i.e., INR 
280,00,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Two Hundred and Eighty Crore only), within the 
initial half or lhe Maximum Buyback Period, except for \he reasons menlloned in the 
Buyback Regulations. the amount held in the Escrow Account (up to a maxjmum of 
2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size) shall be liable to be forfetted and deposited 
in 1he Investor ProlecUon and Education Fund of SEBI or as directed by SEBI In 
accordance with the Buyback, Regulations. 
FIRM FINANCING ARRANGEMENTS 
The Company, duly authorised by the Board. has identified and earmarked funds 
ror the.purpose of fulfilment of its obllgallons under the Buyback. Such earmarked 
funds are in excess of the Maximum Buyback Size. 
Based on the resolution of the Board dated November 23, 2026 and other facts/ 
documents. Rohit J Aggarwal & Co., Chartered Accountants (Firm Registration No. 
018883N), through Its partner, RohJt Aggar,val (Membership No. 096511). have 
certified, v/tJe their letter dated November 23, 2026, that the Company has made 
firm financing arrangements for fulfilling its obligations under the Buyback. 
The Manager to lhe Buyback, having regard to the above, confirms that firm 
arrangements for fulfilling the obligations under the Buyback are in place.

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS ON THE LIKELY IMPACT 
OF THE BUYBACK ON THE COMPANY 
The management believes that the Buyback Is expected to Improve return on 
equity and earnings per share by reduction in the equity base, thereby leading to 
an Increase in shareholders' value over lhe long lerm. The amount required for lhe 
Buyback (Including Transaction Costs) will be funded out of the free reserves and/ 
or the securities premium account of the Company and/ or such other sources as 
may be petmltted by law, and the Buyback is not expected to Impact the growth 
opportunilies or the Company In any malarial way, save for a reducllon in the 
treasury Income lhat the Company could olheiwise have earned on lhe funds 
deployed. 
Pursuant to Regulation 16(ii) of the Buyback Regulations, the Promoler Group is 
not entitled to participate in the Buyback. The Buyback will not result in a change 
in control or otherwise affect the existing management structure of the Company. 
The aggregale shareholding or Ille Promoter Group as on the date or this Public 
Announcement Is 55.84% of the tolal equity share-capital or the Company. 
Depending on the number of Equity Shares boughl back. the percentage 
shareholding of the Promoter Group will Increase. However, total voling rights held 
by the Promoter Group post-Buyback will not exceed the maximum permissible 
non-publlc shareholding. The exact percentage of voting rights of the Promoter 
Group, post Buyback will be dependent upon the actual number or Eqully 
Shares bought back under the Boyback. Further, the promoters of the Company 
already have control over lhe affairs of the Company and therefore any Increase 
in voting rights of the Promoter Group consequent 10 Buyback, will not result in 
any change in controi over the affairs of1he Company and shall be in oompliance 
with the provisions of the Securities and Exchange Board of India (Substantial 
Acquisition of Shares and Takeovers) Regulations. 2011. as amended and the 
Listing Regulations. Such increase is not an active acquisition. is incidental to the 
Buyback and falls within the limits prescribed under the Securities .and Exchange 
Board of India (Substantial Acquisition of Shares and Takeovers) Regulations, 
2011. Public shareholding shall not fall below 25% of the total fully paid,up equity 
share capital of the Company. 
As required under Section 68(2)(d) of the Companies Act and Regulation 4(1i) of 
the Buyback Regulations, the ratio of the aggregate or secured and unsecured 
debts owed by the Company shall nol be more than twfce the paW-up equity 
share capita! and free reserves after the Buyback. based on the standalone or 
consolidated financial statements of the Company, whichever Is lower. 
The Company shall not raise further capital for a period of 1 (one) year from the 
expiry of the Buyback period, except in discharge of its subsisting obligations, and 
shall not issue any Equity Shares or other specified securities (including by way 
of bonus) till the expiry of Ille Buyback period, except in discharge of subsisting 
obtigations. The Company shall transfer from its free reserves a sum equal to the 
nominal value of the Equity Shares bought back to the capital redemption reserve 
aocount.

20 
DETAILS OF ESCROW ACCOUNT ANO THE AMOUNT DEPOSITED 
THEREIN 
20.1 
In accordance with Regulation 20 of the Buyback Regulations and towards security 
for performance of Its obligations thereunder, the Company has entered into an 
escrow agreement dated November 25, 2026 (·Escrow Agreement' ) with the 
Manager to the Buyback and Axis Bank Limited, which has been appointed as 
the escrow bank ("Escrow Bank•), pursuant to which the Company has opened 
an escrow account In the name and Style "DALMIA BHARAT LIMITED-ESCROW 
ACCOUNT -BUYBACK 2026" ("Escrow Account"). The Company has authorised 
the Manager to the Buyback lo operate the Escrow Account in compliance with 
the Buyback Regulations and the Escrow Agreement. The Company will, within 
2 (two) Working Days from the dale of this Public Announcement, in accordance 
with Regulation 20(i) of the Buyback Regulations, deposit a sum equivalent to 
2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size in the Escrow Account and arrange for a 
bank guarantee in favour of the Manager to the Buyback, which together with the 
cash deposited In the Escrow Account makes up the requisite escrow amount 
under the Buyback Regulations being 25% of the Maximum Buyback Size 
("Escrow Amount"). Further, such bank guarantee will be valid (i) for a period of 
30 (thirty) days after the expiry of the Buyback Period, or (ii) till the completion of 
all obligations of the Company under the Buyback Regulations, whichever is later. 
20.2 
The funds in the Escrow Account may be released for making payment to the 
shareholders, subject to at least 2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size, i.e. INR 
17,50,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Seventeen Crore Fifty lakh only) remaining In 
the Escrow Account at all points In time. The balance lying to the credit of the 
Escrow Account will be released to the Company in compliance with the Buyback 
Regulations. 
20.3 
If the Company Is unable to complete the Buyback equivalent to the Minimum 
Buyback Size, I.e., INR 525,00,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Five Hundred and Twenty 
Flve Crore only) or to utilise at least 40% of the Maximum Buyback Size, i.e., INR 
280,00,00,0001- (Indian Rupees Two Hundred and Eighty Crore only), within the 
Initial half of the Maximum Buyback Period, except for the reasons mentioned In the 
Buyback Regulations, the amount held in the Escrow Account (up to a maximum of 
2.5% of the Maximum Buyback Size) shall be liable to be forfeited and deposited 
in the Investor Protection and Education Fund of SEBI or as directed by SEBI In 
accordance with the Buyback Regulations. 
21 
FIRM FINANCING ARRANGEMENTS 
21.1 
The Company, duly authorised by the Board, has identified and earmarked funds 
for the purpose of fulfilment of its obligations under lhe Buyback. Such earmarked 
funds are in excess of the Maximum Buyback Size. 
21.2 
Based on the resolution of the Board dated November 23, 2026 and other facts/ 
documents, Rohit J Aggarwal & Co., Chartered Accountants (Firm Registration No. 
018883N), through its partner, Rohit Aggarwal (Membership No. 096511), have 
certified, vide their letter dated November 23, 2026, that the Company has made 
firm financing arrangements for fulfilling its obligations under the Buyback. 
21.3 
The Manager to the Buyback, having regard to the above, confirms that firm 
arrangements for fulfilling the obligations under the Buyback are in place.

25 
MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS ON THE LIKELY IMPACT 
OFTHEBUYBACKONTHECOMPANY 
25.1 
The management believes that the Buyback is expected to Improve return on 
equity and earnings per share by reduction in the equity base, thereby leading to 
an increase in shareholders' value over the long term. The amount required for the 
Buyback (including Transaction Costs) will be funded out of the free reserves and/ 
or the securities premium account of the Company and/ or such other sources as 
may be permitted by law, and the Buyback Is not expected l.o impact the growth 
opportunities of the Company in any material way, save for a reduction in the 
treasury income that the Company could otherwise have earned on the funds 
deployed. 
25.2 
Pursuant to Regulation 16(ii) of the Buyback Regulations. the Promoter Group is 
not entitled to participate in the Buyback. The Buyback will not result In a change 
in control or otherwise affect the existing management structure of the Company. 
25.3 
The aggregate shareholding of the Promoter Group as on the date of this Public 
Announcement is 55.84% of the total equity share capital of the Company. 
Depending on the number of Equity Shares bought back, the percentage 
shareholding of the Promoter Group will Increase. However, total voting rights held 
by the Promoter Group post-Buyback wlll not exceed the maximum permissible 
non-public shareholding. The exact percentage of voting rights of the Promoter 
Group, post Buyback will be dependent upon the actual number of Equity 
Shares bought back under the Buyback. Further, the promoters of the Company 
already have control over the affairs of the Company and therefore any increase 
In voting rights of the Promoter Group consequent to Buyback, will not result in 
any change In control over the affairs of the Company and shall be in compliance 
with the provisions of the Securities and Exchange Board of India (Substantial 
Acquisition of Shares and Takeovers) Regulations, 2011, as amended and the 
Listing Regulations. Such increase is not an active acquisition, Is Incidental lo the 
Buyback and falls within the limits prescribed under the Securities and Exchange 
Board of India (Substantial Acquisition of Shares and Takeovers) Regulations, 
2011 . Public sharehOlding shall not fall below 25% of the total fully paid-up equity 
share capital of the Company. 
25.4 
As required under Section 68(2}(d) of the Companies Act and Regulation 4(ii) of 
the Buyback Regulalions, the ratio of the aggregate of secured and unsecured 
debts owed by the Company shall not be more than twice the paid-up equity 
share capital and free reserves after the Buyback, based on the standalone or 
consolidated financial statements of the Company, whichever Is lower. 
25.5 
The Company shall not raise further capital for a period of 1 {one) year from the 
expiry of the Buyback period, except in discharge of its subsisting obligations, and 
shall not issue any Equity Shares or other specified securities (including by way 
or bonus) HII the expiry of the Buyback period, except in discharge of subsisting 
obligations. The Company shall transfer from its free reserves a sum equal to the 
nominal value of the Equity Shares bought back to the capital redemption reserve 
account.

CERTIFIED TRUE COPY OF THE RESOLUTIONS PASSED AT THE MEETING OF THE BOARD OF 
DIRECTORS OF DALMIA BHARAT LIMITED (THE "COMPANY") HELD AT TEJAS CONFERENCE 
ROOM, 6TH FLOOR, HT HOUSE, KASTURBA GANDHI MARG, NEW DELHI – 110 001 ON 
MONDAY, NOVEMBER 23, 2026. 
“RESOLVED THAT in accordance with Article 29 of the Articles of Association of the Company, 
pursuant to the provisions of Sections 68, 69, 70 and all other applicable provisions, if any, of 
the Companies Act, 2013, as amended (“Companies Act”), the Companies (Share Capital and 
Debentures) Rules, 2014, as amended (“Share Capital Rules”), the Companies (Management 
and Administration) Rules, 2014, as amended (“Management Rules”), to the extent 
applicable, the Securities and Exchange Board of India (Listing Obligations and Disclosure 
Requirements) Regulations, 2015, as amended ("LODR Regulations”) and the Securities and 
Exchange Board of India (Buy-Back of Securities) Regulations, 2018, as amended (“Buyback 
Regulations”), and any statutory modification(s) or re-enactment thereof, for the time being 
in force and, subject to such other approvals, permissions and sanctions of the Securities and 
Exchange Board of India (“SEBI”), Registrar of Companies (“ROC”), BSE Limited ("BSE"), 
National Stock Exchange of India Limited ("NSE”, together with BSE referred to as the “Stock 
Exchanges”) and/ or other authorities, institutions or bodies (the “Appropriate Authorities”), 
as may be necessary/ applicable and subject to such conditions and modifications as may be 
prescribed by the Appropriate Authorities or imposed while granting such approvals, 
permissions, sanctions and exemptions which may be agreed to by the Board of Directors of 
the Company (the "Board" which expression shall include any committee constituted by the 
Board to exercise its powers, including the powers conferred by this resolution), the Board 
hereby approves the buyback by the Company of its fully paid-up equity shares, having face 
value of INR 2/- (Indian Rupees Two only) each ("Equity Shares") at a price not exceeding INR 
1,850 (Indian Rupees One Thousand Eight Hundred and Fifty only) per Equity Share 
("Maximum Buyback Price") and for an aggregate amount not exceeding INR 700,00,00,000/- 
(Indian Rupees Seven Hundred Crore only) ("Maximum Buyback Size"), from the shareholders 
of the Company/ beneficial owners of the Equity Shares excluding promoters, members of the 
promoter group and persons who are in control of the Company, payable in cash via the "open 
market" route through the Stock Exchanges, under the Buyback Regulations and the 
Companies Act; 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT at the Maximum Buyback Price and for the Maximum Buyback 
Size, the indicative maximum number of Equity Shares bought back would be 37,83,783 
(Thirty Seven Lakh Eighty Three Thousand Seven Hundred and Eighty Three) ("Maximum 
Buyback Shares") which is approx. 2.02% of the total number of paid-up Equity Shares of the 
Company. If the Equity Shares are bought back at a price below the Maximum Buyback Price, 
the actual number of Equity Shares bought back could exceed the indicative Maximum 
Buyback Shares (assuming full deployment of Maximum Buyback Size) but will always be 
subject to the Maximum Buyback Size (the process hereinafter referred to as the "Buyback"). 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Buyback shall not exceed the Maximum Buyback Size, which 
represents 9.64% and 4.49% of the aggregate of the total paid-up equity share capital and free 
reserves (including securities premium account) of the Company based on the latest audited 
standalone and consolidated financial statements of the Company as on March 31, 2026, 
respectively, and is (i) within the statutory limit of 10% of the aggregate of the fully paid-up 
Annexure -2

---


## Page 19

2 | 11 
equity share capital and free reserves of the Company, based on the standalone or 
consolidated financial statements of the Company, whichever sets out a lower amount, under 
the Board approval route under the Companies Act and the Buyback Regulations; and (ii) less 
than 15% of the paid-up capital and free reserves of the Company based on both standalone 
and consolidated financial statements of the Company, as prescribed under the Buyback 
Regulations for buyback from the open market through the Stock Exchanges. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of Regulation 15 of the Buyback Regulations, the Company 
shall utilise at least 75% (seventy-five percent) of the Maximum Buyback Size i.e., INR 
525,00,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Five Hundred and Twenty Five Crore only) ("Minimum 
Buyback Size") towards the Buyback, and shall utilise at least 40% (forty percent) of the 
Maximum Buyback Size i.e., INR 280,00,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Two Hundred and Eighty 
Crore only) within the initial half of the 66 (sixty-six) working days from the date of opening 
of the Buyback, and accordingly, based on the Maximum Buyback Price and Minimum Buyback 
Size, the Company will purchase an indicative minimum number of 28,37,837 (Twenty Eight 
Lakh Thirty Seven Thousand Eight Hundred and Thirty Seven) Equity Shares.  
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of the Buyback Regulations, in the event of non-
compliance with Regulation 15 of the Buyback Regulations, SEBI may direct the Manager (as 
defined below) to the Buyback to forfeit the escrow account, subject to a maximum of 2.5% 
of the Maximum Buyback Size, except in the circumstances specified in the Buyback 
Regulations, and the amount so forfeited shall be deposited in the Investor Protection and 
Education Fund of SEBI.  
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Maximum Buyback Size shall not include any expenses incurred 
or to be incurred for the Buyback such as filing fees payable to SEBI, advisors’ fees, Stock 
Exchanges’ fee for usage of their platform for Buyback, fees payable to depositories, 
brokerage, applicable taxes such as securities transaction tax, Goods and Services Tax, 
buyback tax, stamp duty, etc., public announcement publication expenses, intermediaries’ 
fees, printing and dispatch expenses and other incidental and related expenses. The Buyback 
period shall commence from the date of this Board resolution approving the Buyback until the 
date on which the payment of consideration for the Equity Shares bought back by the 
Company is made (“Buyback Period”), in accordance with, and in consonance with, the 
provisions contained in the Buyback Regulations, the Companies Act, the Share Capital Rules, 
the Management Rules and the LODR Regulations. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in accordance with the Buyback Regulations, such Buyback may be 
made out of the Company’s securities premium account, free reserves and/or such other 
source as may be permitted by law (but not out of the proceeds of an earlier issue of the same 
kind of shares or other specified securities) through the methodology of "open market 
purchases through Stock Exchanges", as provided in the Buyback Regulations, and on such 
terms and conditions as the Board may deem fit. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Company has complied and shall continue to comply with 
Section 70 of the Companies Act, wherein: 
 
a) It shall not directly or indirectly purchase its own shares:

---


## Page 20

3 | 11 
i. 
through any subsidiary company including its own subsidiary companies; or 
ii. 
through any investment company or group of investment companies. 
 
b) There are no defaults subsisting in the repayment of deposits, interest payment 
thereon, redemption of debentures or interest payment thereon or redemption of 
preference shares or payment of dividend due to any shareholder, or repayment of 
any term loans or interest payable thereon to any financial institution or banking 
company, and in case of defaults which have ceased to subsist, if any, a period of more 
than 3 (three) years has lapsed. 
 
c) The Company is in compliance with the provisions of Sections 92, 123, 127 and 129 of 
the Companies Act. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Board hereby confirms that: 
 
a) all Equity Shares of the Company are fully paid-up; 
 
b) the Company has not undertaken a buyback of any of its securities during the period 
of 1 (one) year immediately preceding the date of this Board meeting; 
 
c) the Company shall not issue and allot any shares or other specified securities 
(including by way of bonus), from the date of this Board resolution till the expiry of 
the Buyback Period, except in discharge of subsisting obligations through conversion 
of warrants, stock option schemes, sweat equity or conversion of preference shares 
or debentures into Equity Shares, the relevant details and potential impact of which, 
if any, shall be disclosed in the public announcement; 
 
d) the Company shall not raise further capital for a period of 1 (one) year from the expiry 
of the Buyback Period, except in discharge of its subsisting obligations; 
 
e) consideration for the Equity Shares bought back by the Company will be paid only by 
way of cash; 
 
f) the Company shall not buy back its shares or other specified securities from any 
person through negotiated deals whether on or off the Stock Exchanges or through 
spot transactions or through any private arrangement; 
 
g) the Maximum Buyback Size i.e., INR 700,00,00,000/- (Indian Rupees Seven Hundred 
Crore only), does not exceed (i) 10% of the aggregate of the paid-up equity share 
capital and free reserves, and (ii) 15% of the paid-up capital and free reserves of the 
Company, each as per the latest audited standalone and consolidated financial 
statements of the Company for the financial year ended March 31, 2026 (i.e., the last 
audited financial statements available as on the date of the Board meeting); 
 
h) if the Equity Shares are bought back at a price below the Maximum Buyback Price, the 
actual number of Equity Shares bought back could exceed the indicative Maximum 
Buyback Shares (assuming full deployment of Maximum Buyback Size) but will always 
be subject to the Maximum Buyback Size. The number of Equity Shares to be bought

---


## Page 21

4 | 11 
back will not exceed 25% of the total number of Equity Shares in the total paid-up 
Equity Share capital of the Company as per the latest audited standalone or 
consolidated financial statements of the Company as on March 31, 2026, whichever 
sets out a lower amount; 
 
i) 
the ratio of the aggregate of secured and unsecured debts owed by the Company to 
the paid-up capital and free reserves after the Buyback shall not be more than 2:1, 
based on the standalone or consolidated financial statements of the Company, 
whichever is lower, as prescribed under the Companies Act and Buyback Regulations; 
 
j) 
the Company shall not undertake another buyback within a period of 1 (one) year 
reckoned from the expiry of the Buyback Period, in terms of the Buyback Regulations; 
 
k) there is no pendency of any scheme of amalgamation or compromise or arrangement 
involving the Company pursuant to the provisions of the Companies Act as on date; 
 
l) 
the Company shall not withdraw the Buyback after the public announcement in 
relation to the Buyback is made, in terms of the Buyback Regulations;  
 
m) the Company is not undertaking the Buyback so as to delist its Equity Shares or other 
specified securities from the Stock Exchanges; 
 
n) the Buyback shall not result in a breach of the minimum public shareholding 
requirements prescribed under the Securities Contracts (Regulation) Rules, 1957 or 
the LODR Regulations, in terms of the Buyback Regulations;  
 
o) in terms of Section 69 of the Companies Act, the Company shall transfer from its free 
reserves or securities premium account and/ or such sources as may be permitted 
by law, a sum equal to the nominal value of the Equity Shares purchased through 
the Buyback to the capital redemption reserve account and the details of such 
transfer shall be disclosed in its subsequent audited balance sheet; 
 
p) the Buyback shall not be made from the promoters, promoter group or persons in 
control of the Company; 
 
q) the Buyback will be implemented by the Company by way of open market purchases 
through the Stock Exchanges having nationwide trading terminals, only through the 
order matching mechanism except "all or none" order matching system, in respect of 
frequently traded shares and subject to the restrictions on placement of bids, price 
and volume as may be specified by SEBI, as provided under the Buyback Regulations; 
 
r) the Company shall submit the information regarding the Equity Shares bought back 
by it to the Stock Exchanges on a daily basis in accordance with the Buyback 
Regulations and in such form as may be prescribed by SEBI so as to enable the Stock 
Exchanges to upload the same on their website immediately. The Company shall also 
upload the information regarding Equity Shares bought back on its website on a daily 
basis;

---


## Page 22

5 | 11 
s) the promoters and members of the promoter group of the Company, and their 
associates, shall not deal in the Equity Shares or other specified securities of the 
Company either through the Stock Exchanges or off-market transactions (including 
inter-se transfer of Equity Shares among the promoters and members of the promoter 
group) from the date of this Board resolution till the closing of the Buyback offer, and 
the Equity Shares held by them shall remain frozen at the ISIN level during such 
period, and the Company shall provide necessary instructions to the depositories for 
giving effect to such freeze; 
 
t) as on the date of this Resolution, the Company does not have any lender, as such, 
there is no need of obtaining any prior consent of the lenders; 
 
u) the Company shall ensure consequent reduction of its share capital post Buyback, and 
the Equity Shares bought back by the Company will be extinguished and physically 
destroyed (if applicable) in the manner prescribed under the Buyback Regulations and 
the Companies Act within the specified timelines; 
 
v) the Company shall earmark and make arrangements for adequate sources of funds 
for the purpose of the Buyback in accordance with the Buyback Regulations; 
 
w) the Company shall comply with the statutory and regulatory timelines in respect of 
the Buyback in such manner as prescribed under the Companies Act and/ or the 
Buyback Regulations and any other applicable laws; 
 
x) the information pertaining to the Company as may be set out in the public 
announcement and any other advertisement, corrigendum or documents with 
respect to the Buyback shall be true, factual and material and shall not contain any 
misleading information, and the directors of the Company accept responsibility for 
the information contained therein. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Board hereby confirms that it has made a full enquiry into the 
affairs and prospects of the Company and that based on such full enquiry conducted into the 
affairs and prospects of the Company, the Board has formed an opinion that: 
 
a) immediately following the date of this Board meeting, there will be no grounds on 
which the Company could be found unable to pay its debts; 
 
b) as regards the Company’s prospects for the year immediately following the date of 
this Board meeting, and having regard to the Board’s intention with respect to the 
management of the Company’s business during that year and to the amount and 
character of the financial resources which will, in the Board’s view, be available to the 
Company during that year, the Company will be able to meet its liabilities as and when 
they fall due and will not be rendered insolvent within a period of one year from the 
date of this Board meeting; and 
 
c) in forming an opinion as aforesaid, the Board has taken into account the liabilities, as 
if the Company were being wound up under the provisions of the Companies Act,

---


## Page 23

6 | 11 
1956 or the Companies Act, or the Insolvency and Bankruptcy Code, 2016, as 
applicable (including prospective and contingent liabilities). 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Company shall not use borrowed funds, directly or indirectly, 
whether secured or unsecured, of any form and nature, from banks and financial institutions 
for paying the consideration for the Equity Shares bought back in the Buyback. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Company shall not buy back the locked-in Equity Shares or 
other specified securities, if any, and non-transferable Equity Shares or other specified 
securities, if any, till the pendency of the lock-in or till the Equity Shares or other specified 
securities become transferable, in terms of the Buyback Regulations, and in terms of 
Regulation 40(1) of the LODR Regulations, the Company shall not buy back Equity Shares held 
in physical form until such Equity Shares are dematerialised. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Buyback from shareholders who are persons resident outside 
India including the foreign institutional investors/ foreign portfolio investors, overseas 
corporate bodies and shareholders of foreign nationality, if any, shall be subject to such 
approvals, if, and to the extent necessary or required including approvals from concerned 
authorities including approvals from the Reserve Bank of India under the Foreign Exchange 
Management Act, 1999, and the rules and regulations framed thereunder, if any, to be 
procured by such shareholders resident outside India.  
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT as per the provisions of Section 68(8) of the Companies Act, the 
Company will not make a further issue of the same kind of shares or other securities including 
allotment of new shares under clause (a) of sub-section (1) of Section 62 of the Companies 
Act or other specified securities within a period of 6 (six) months after the completion of the 
Buyback except by way of bonus issue or in the discharge of subsisting obligations such as 
conversion of warrants, stock option schemes, sweat equity or conversion of preference 
shares or debentures into Equity Shares. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT as required under the Companies Act, the draft of the declaration 
of solvency (“Declaration”) along with annexures thereof prepared in the prescribed form and 
verified by an affidavit, placed before the meeting be and is hereby approved upon making 
full enquiry into the affairs of the Company such that the Board is satisfied that the Company 
is capable of meeting its liabilities and will not be rendered insolvent within a period of one 
year from the date of such Declaration; and Mr. Puneet Yadu Dalmia, Managing Director & 
CEO and Ms. Anuradha Mookerjee, Director of the Company, be and are hereby jointly 
authorised to finalise and sign the same, for and on behalf of the Board, and Mr. Rajeev Kumar, 
Company Secretary, be and is hereby authorised to file the same with the ROC and SEBI before 
making the Buyback.  
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Board hereby takes on record the draft report to be issued by 
Walker Chandiok & Co LLP, the statutory auditors of the Company, as required under clause 
(xi) of Schedule I of the Buyback Regulations. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of the Buyback Regulations, the Company shall make a 
public announcement within 2 (two) working days from the date of this Board resolution, 
containing the disclosures specified in Schedule IV of the Buyback Regulations, and shall

---


## Page 24

7 | 11 
simultaneously file a copy thereof in electronic mode with SEBI (along with the fees specified 
in Schedule V of the Buyback Regulations) and the Stock Exchanges, and shall, within 1 (one) 
working day from the date of the public announcement, send an intimation through electronic 
mode regarding the Buyback to the persons who were its shareholders as on the date of the 
public announcement. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of the Buyback Regulations, a copy of this resolution be 
filed with SEBI and the Stock Exchanges within 2 (two) working days of the date of passing of 
this resolution, and all filings with SEBI shall be made in electronic mode after being digitally 
signed by the Company Secretary or any person authorised by the Board, in terms of 
Regulation 5(ix) of the Buyback Regulations. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of the Buyback Regulations, Mr. Rajeev Kumar, Company 
Secretary, be and is hereby appointed as the Compliance Officer for the implementation of 
the Buyback and Mr. Williams R. of the Registrar (as defined below) be and is hereby 
designated as the person responsible for investors’ services to redress the grievances of the 
investors. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT Mr. Yatin Malhotra, Chief Financial Officer and Mr. Rajeev Kumar, 
Company Secretary be and are hereby jointly authorised to finalise the public announcement 
in consultation with the merchant banker and to sign the public announcement and 
corrigendum thereto, if any, on behalf of the Company. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in terms of the Buyback Regulations, the Buyback shall open within 
4 (four) working days from the date of the public announcement and shall close within 66 
(sixty six) working days from the date of opening of the Buyback, or such other period as may 
be permitted under the Companies Act and/or Buyback Regulations or as may be directed by 
the Appropriate Authorities, and in any event the Buyback shall be completed within 1 (one) 
year from the date of this Board resolution in terms of Section 68(4) of the Companies Act and 
Regulation 5(ii) of the Buyback Regulations. The Board or the Buyback Committee (as defined 
below) may, in its discretion, close the Buyback earlier once the Minimum Buyback Size has 
been utilised, by giving appropriate notice of such earlier closure and completing all 
formalities in this regard. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Company shall maintain a register of securities bought back 
wherein details of Equity Shares bought back, consideration paid for the Equity Shares bought 
back, date of cancellation of Equity Shares and date of extinguishing and physically destroying 
of Equity Shares and such other particulars as may be prescribed, shall be entered, in terms 
of the Companies Act and of the Buyback Regulations, and that Mr. Rajeev Kumar, Company 
Secretary be and is hereby authorised to authenticate the entries made in the said register. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Company shall, after the expiry of the Buyback Period, file with 
the ROC and SEBI a return containing the particulars relating to the Buyback within 30 (thirty) 
days of such expiry, in terms of the Companies Act and of the Buyback Regulations, and shall, 
within 2 (two) working days of expiry of the Buyback Period, issue a public advertisement in a 
national daily in terms of Regulation 24(vi) of the Buyback Regulations.

## Page 25

8 | 11 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT nothing contained herein shall confer any right on any shareholder 
to offer and/ or any obligation on the Company or the Board or the Buyback Committee to 
buy back any shares and / or impair any power of the Company or the Board or the Buyback 
Committee to terminate any process in relation to such Buyback, if so permissible by law. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT Mr. Rajeev Kumar, Company Secretary be and is hereby authorised 
to file necessary e-forms with the ROC and to do all such acts, deeds and things incidental 
thereto, including signing and filing of forms, payment of fees, etc. and to do all such other 
acts, things and deeds, as may be required for the aforesaid purpose or as may be necessary 
to give effect to the above resolutions. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT Mr. Rajeev Kumar, Company Secretary be and is hereby authorised 
to represent the Company before the ROC, SEBI, the Stock Exchanges or any other agencies 
connected with the Buyback of the Company and to sign and submit all forms, letters, 
documents or other papers that may be required for the implementation of the Buyback. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT Mr. Rajeev Kumar, Company Secretary and Mr. Yatin Malhotra, 
Chief Financial Officer be and are hereby severally authorised to send the necessary 
intimations to the Stock Exchanges in relation to the Buyback, as may be required under the 
LODR Regulations and the Buyback Regulations. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in compliance with the Buyback Regulations, the approval of the 
Board be and is hereby accorded for appointment of Axis Capital Limited, a SEBI registered 
merchant banker, as manager to the Buyback (“Manager”) and for other services related to 
the Buyback at such fees and other terms and conditions as mutually agreed with them. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in compliance with the Buyback Regulations, the approval of the 
Board be and is hereby also accorded for appointment of Axis Capital Limited as broker to the 
Buyback and for other services related to the Buyback at such fees and other terms and 
conditions as mutually agreed with them. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT in compliance with the Buyback Regulations, M/s. Kfin 
Technologies Limited be and is hereby appointed as the registrar to the Buyback (“Registrar”) 
at such remuneration as mutually agreed with them and is hereby designated as the investors 
service centre, as required under Regulation 24(iii) of the Buyback Regulations. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the appointment of M/s Khaitan & Co as the legal advisors to the 
Company for the Buyback be and is hereby ratified at such fees and other terms and conditions 
as mutually agreed. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT a committee be constituted (“Buyback Committee”) comprising 
Mr. Gautam Dalmia, Managing Director, Mr. Puneet Yadu Dalmia, Managing Director & CEO, 
Mr. Yatin Malhotra, Chief Financial Officer, Mr. Dharmender Tuteja and Mr. Rajeev Kumar, 
Company Secretary and that the Buyback Committee is hereby authorised to do all such acts, 
deeds, matters and things as it may, in its absolute discretion, deem necessary or expedient, 
and as the Buyback Committee may consider to be in the best interests of the shareholders, 
including but not limited to the following:

RESOLVED FURTHER THAT Mr. Gautam Dalmia shall be the Chairman of the Committee. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the quorum for any meeting of the Buyback Committee for 
implementing the Buyback shall be any two members and Buyback Committee may regulate 
its own proceedings and meet as often as required, to discharge its functions. The Buyback 
committee may accord approval through circular resolution. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Buyback Committee shall report from time to time to the Board 
at the meeting of the Board, status/ progress of actions taken by the Buyback Committee 
concerning Buyback and the minutes of meeting(s) of the Buyback Committee held in the 
intervening period of two successive meetings of the Board shall be put up at the subsequent 
meeting of the Board. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Buyback Committee shall have the power and authority to 
delegate all or any of the authorities conferred upon it to any officer(s) and/ or representatives 
of the Company, in order to give effect to the aforesaid resolutions and to revoke and 
substitute such delegation / sub-delegation of authority from time to time. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT for the purpose of giving effect to this resolution, subject to 
applicable law, the Board and the Buyback Committee be and is hereby authorised to accept 
and make any alteration(s), modification(s) to the terms and conditions and delegate such 
powers, obligations and responsibilities as it may, and to whomsoever it may deem necessary, 
concerning any aspect of the Buyback, in accordance with the statutory requirements as well 
as to give such directions as may be necessary or desirable, to settle any questions, difficulties 
or doubts that may arise and generally, to do all such acts, deeds, matters and things as it 
may, in its absolute discretion deem necessary, expedient, usual or proper in relation to or in 
connection with or for matters consequential to the Buyback without seeking any further 
consent or approval of the shareholders or otherwise to the end and intent that they shall be 
deemed to have given their approval thereto expressly by the authority of this resolution. 
 
RESOLVED FURTHER THAT the Buyback Committee shall stand dissolved on the date of 
completion of the Buyback process.”
